{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/sqlite-map-parser\n\n# SQLite to Structured JSON\n\nParse SQLite databases by exploring schemas first, then extracting data into structured JSON.\n\n## Step 1: Explore the Schema\n\nAlways start by understanding what tables exist and their structure.\n\n### List All Tables\n```sql\nSELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table';\n```\n\n### Inspect Table Schema\n```sql\n-- Get column names and types\nPRAGMA table_info(TableName);\n\n-- See CREATE statement\nSELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='TableName';\n```\n\n### Find Primary/Unique Keys\n```sql\n-- Primary key info\nPRAGMA table_info(TableName);  -- 'pk' column shows primary key order\n\n-- All indexes (includes unique constraints)\nPRAGMA index_list(TableName);\n\n-- Columns in an index\nPRAGMA index_info(index_name);\n```\n\n## Step 2: Understand Relationships\n\n### Identify Foreign Keys\n```sql\nPRAGMA foreign_key_list(TableName);\n```\n\n### Common Patterns\n\n**ID-based joins:** Tables often share an ID column\n```sql\n-- Main table has ID as primary key\n-- Related tables reference it\nSELECT m.*, r.ExtraData\nFROM MainTable m\nLEFT JOIN RelatedTable r ON m.ID = r.ID;\n```\n\n**Coordinate-based keys:** Spatial data often uses computed coordinates\n```python\n# If ID represents a linear index into a grid:\nx = id % width\ny = id // width\n```\n\n## Step 3: Extract and Transform\n\n### Basic Pattern\n```python\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\n\ndef parse_sqlite_to_json(db_path):\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row  # Access columns by name\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n\n    # 1. Explore schema\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\")\n    tables = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]\n\n    # 2. Get dimensions/metadata from config table\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM MetadataTable LIMIT 1\")\n    metadata = dict(cursor.fetchone())\n\n    # 3. Build indexed data structure\n    data = {}\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM MainTable\")\n    for row in cursor.fetchall():\n        key = row[\"ID\"]  # or compute: (row[\"X\"], row[\"Y\"])\n        data[key] = dict(row)\n\n    # 4. Join related data\n    cursor.execute(\"SELECT * FROM RelatedTable\")\n    for row in cursor.fetchall():\n        key = row[\"ID\"]\n        if key in data:\n            data[key][\"extra_field\"] = row[\"Value\"]\n\n    conn.close()\n    return {\"metadata\": metadata, \"items\": list(data.values())}\n```\n\n### Handle Missing Tables Gracefully\n```python\ndef safe_query(cursor, query):\n    try:\n        cursor.execute(query)\n        return cursor.fetchall()\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        return []  # Table doesn't exist\n```\n\n## Step 4: Output as Structured JSON\n\n### Map/Dictionary Output\nUse when items have natural unique keys:\n```json\n{\n  \"metadata\": {\"width\": 44, \"height\": 26},\n  \"tiles\": {\n    \"0,0\": {\"terrain\": \"GRASS\", \"feature\": null},\n    \"1,0\": {\"terrain\": \"PLAINS\", \"feature\": \"FOREST\"},\n    \"2,0\": {\"terrain\": \"COAST\", \"resource\": \"FISH\"}\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Array Output\nUse when order matters or keys are simple integers:\n```json\n{\n  \"metadata\": {\"width\": 44, \"height\": 26},\n  \"tiles\": [\n    {\"x\": 0, \"y\": 0, \"terrain\": \"GRASS\"},\n    {\"x\": 1, \"y\": 0, \"terrain\": \"PLAINS\", \"feature\": \"FOREST\"},\n    {\"x\": 2, \"y\": 0, \"terrain\": \"COAST\", \"resource\": \"FISH\"}\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n## Common Schema Patterns\n\n### Grid/Map Data\n- Main table: positions with base properties\n- Feature tables: join on position ID for overlays\n- Compute (x, y) from linear ID: `x = id % width, y = id // width`\n\n### Hierarchical Data\n- Parent table with primary key\n- Child tables with foreign key reference\n- Use LEFT JOIN to preserve all parents\n\n### Enum/Lookup Tables\n- Type tables map codes to descriptions\n- Join to get human-readable values\n\n## Debugging Tips\n\n```sql\n-- Sample data from any table\nSELECT * FROM TableName LIMIT 5;\n\n-- Count rows\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM TableName;\n\n-- Find distinct values in a column\nSELECT DISTINCT ColumnName FROM TableName;\n\n-- Check for nulls\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM TableName WHERE ColumnName IS NULL;\n```\n\n\nARGUMENTS: --db_path /data/maps/e2e_test_case_0.Civ6Map"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6570b78e-a433-4c2e-9839-024a8543cc00","uuid":"610f3c34-2b45-4b1c-925e-334e030f7894","isSynthetic":true}

Place Commercial Hubs on coastal tiles with most water adjacency\n# But not on tiles already used for Harbors\ncommercial_candidates = []\nfor tile in coastal_tiles:\n    if tile in placed_districts:\n        continue\n    water_count = sum(1 for n in tile['neighbors'] if n['terrain'] in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN'])\n    score = water_count\n    commercial_candidates.append((score, tile))\n\ncommercial_candidates.sort(reverse=True)\ncommercial_hubs_to_place = min(2, len(commercial_candidates))\n\nprint(f\"\\nPlacing {commercial_hubs_to_place} Commercial Hubs:\")\nfor i in range(commercial_hubs_to_place):\n    score, tile = commercial_candidates[i]\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    tile['planned_district'] = 'COMMERCIAL_HUB'\n    placed_districts.append(tile)\n    print(f\"  COMMERCIAL_HUB at ({x},{y}) - score {score}\")\n\n# 3. Place Industrial Zones on floodplains or tiles with most adjacent districts\nindustrial_candidates = []\nfor tile in available_tiles:\n    if tile in placed_districts:\n        continue\n    score = tile['adjacency_bonuses']['industrial']\n    industrial_candidates.append((score, tile))\n\nindustrial_candidates.sort(reverse=True)\nindustrial_zones_to_place = min(2, len(industrial_candidates))\n\nprint(f\"\\nPlacing {industrial_zones_to_place} Industrial Zones:\")\nfor i in range(industrial_zones_to_place):\n    score, tile = industrial_candidates[i]\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    tile['planned_district'] = 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE'\n    placed_districts.append(tile)\n    print(f\"  INDUSTRIAL_ZONE at ({x},{y}) - base score {score}\")\n\n# 4. Place Campuses on forest tiles or high-adjacency tiles\ncampus_candidates = []\nfor tile in available_tiles:\n    if tile in placed_districts:\n        continue\n    score = tile['adjacency_bonuses']['campus']\n    if 'FOREST' in str(tile['features']) or 'JUNGLE' in str(tile['features']):\n        score += 2  # Bonus for woods/jungle\n    campus_candidates.append((score, tile))\n\ncampus_candidates.sort(reverse=True)\ncampuses_to_place = min(2, len(campus_candidates))\n\nprint(f\"\\nPlacing {campuses_to_place} Campuses:\")\nfor i in range(campuses_to_place):\n    score, tile = campus_candidates[i]\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    tile['planned_district'] = 'CAMPUS'\n    placed_districts.append(tile)\n    print(f\"  CAMPUS at ({x},{y}) - score {score}\")\n\n# 5. Place remaining districts (Theater Squares, Entertainment Complexes, etc.)\nremaining = 9 - len(placed_districts)\nother_candidates = []\nfor tile in available_tiles:\n    if tile in placed_districts:\n        continue\n    score = tile['adjacency_bonuses']['theater']\n    other_candidates.append((score, tile))\n\nother_candidates.sort(reverse=True)\n\nprint(f\"\\nPlacing {remaining} additional districts:\")\nfor i in range(remaining):\n    score, tile = other_candidates[i]\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    tile['planned_district'] = 'THEATER_SQUARE'  # or other district types\n    placed_districts.append(tile)\n    print(f\"  THEATER_SQUARE at ({x},{y}) - score {score}\")\n\n# Calculate actual adjacency bonuses\nprint(\"\\n\\nCalculating adjacency bonuses...\")\n\ndef calculate_actual_adjacency(tile, placed_districts, city_center):\n    \"\"\"Calculate actual adjacency based on Civ6 rules\"\"\"\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    district_type = tile['planned_district']\n    total = 0\n\n    # Check each neighbor\n    for neighbor in tile['neighbors']:\n        nx, ny = neighbor['x'], neighbor['y']\n\n        # City Center adjacency\n        if nx == city_center[0] and ny == city_center[1]:\n            if district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n                total += 1\n            elif district_type == 'HARBOR':\n                total += 1\n            continue\n\n        # Check for adjacent districts\n        for pd in placed_districts:\n            if pd == tile:  # Skip self\n                continue\n            if pd['coordinates']['x'] == nx and pd['coordinates']['y'] == ny:\n                pd_type = pd['planned_district']\n\n                # Commercial Hub + Harbor synergy\n                if district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB' and pd_type == 'HARBOR':\n                    total += 2\n                elif district_type == 'HARBOR' and pd_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n                    total += 2\n\n                # Industrial Zone adjacent to any district\n                elif district_type == 'INDUSTRIAL_ZONE':\n                    total += 1\n\n                # Theater Square adjacent to any district\n                elif district_type == 'THEATER_SQUARE':\n                    total += 1\n\n                # Campus adjacent to any district\n                elif district_type == 'CAMPUS':\n                    total += 1\n\n    # Water adjacency for Commercial Hub\n    if district_type == 'COMMERCIAL_HUB':\n        water_count = sum(1 for n in tile['neighbors']\n                         if n['terrain'] in ['TERRAIN_COAST', 'TERRAIN_OCEAN'])\n        total += water_count\n\n    return total\n\n# Calculate final adjacency bonuses\ndistrict_bonuses = {}\ntotal_adjacency = 0\n\nfor tile in placed_districts:\n    adj = calculate_actual_adjacency(tile, placed_districts, city_center)\n    total_adjacency += adj\n    district_type = tile['planned_district']\n    if district_type not in district_bonuses:\n        district_bonuses[district_type] = 0\n    district_bonuses[district_type] += adj\n\nprint(\"\\nFinal Placement:\")\nfor tile in placed_districts:\n    x, y = tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']\n    print(f\"  {tile['planned_district']:20s} at ({x:2d},{y:2d})\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nAdjacency Bonuses:\")\nfor district, bonus in sorted(district_bonuses.items()):\n    print(f\"  {district:20s}: {bonus:2d}\")\nprint(f\"\\nTotal Adjacency: {total_adjacency}\")\n\n# Create output JSON\noutput = {\n    \"city_center\": list(city_center),\n    \"placements\": {},\n    \"adjacency_bonuses\": district_bonuses,\n    \"total_adjacency\": total_adjacency\n}\n\nfor tile in placed_districts:\n    district_type = tile['planned_district']\n    coords = [tile['coordinates']['x'], tile['coordinates']['y']]\n    output['placements'][district_type] = coords\n\n# Write to file\nimport os\nos.makedirs('/output', exist_ok=True)\nwith open('/output/scenario_3.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n\nprint(f\"\\nResult saved to /output/scenario_3.json\")\nprint(f\"\\nJSON output:\")\nprint(json.dumps(output, indent=2))\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
